 711:     private ShadowPosition updateProfitLock(ShadowPosition p, TradeSignal s) {
 712:         if (s.getLatestPrice() == null || !priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(s, p.entryTime())) return p;
 713:         ShadowPosition n = profitLockState(p, s.getLatestPrice());
 714:         if (!equalsNullable(p.highest(), n.highest()) || p.profitLockActive() != n.profitLockActive()
 715:                 || !equalsNullable(p.profitLockPrice(), n.profitLockPrice())
 716:                 || p.profitLockState() != n.profitLockState()
 717:                 || !java.util.Objects.equals(p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt(), n.profitLockRebaseStartedAt())) {
 718:             jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE wallet_position_test SET highest_price_usdt=?, profit_lock_active=?, profit_lock_price_usdt=?, profit_lock_state=?, profit_lock_rebase_started_at=? WHERE id=?",
 719:                     n.highest(), n.profitLockActive(), n.profitLockPrice(), n.profitLockState().name(),
 720:                     n.profitLockRebaseStartedAt() == null ? null : Timestamp.from(n.profitLockRebaseStartedAt()), p.positionId());
 721:         }
 722:         return n;
 723:     }
 724: 
 725:     private ShadowPosition profitLockState(ShadowPosition p, BigDecimal price) {
 726:         WalletSettings settings = walletSettings();
 727:         BigDecimal activation = nvl(settings.getProfitLockActivationPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(70));
 728:         BigDecimal highest = p.highest() == null ? price : p.highest().max(price);
 729:         BigDecimal progress = progressPercent(p.entryPrice(), p.takeProfit(), highest);
 730: 
 731:         if (p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 732:                 && progress.compareTo(activation) < 0) {
 733:             // Replay parity with Production FIX-118: preserve the historical lock/legacy flag,
 734:             // but keep it non-executable until the new TP geometry earns activation again.
 735:             return p.withLock(highest, true, p.profitLockPrice(), ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE,
 736:                     p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt());
 737:         }
 738: 
 739:         // FIX-118 parity: when REBASE earns activation, rebuild the lock from the current
 740:         // TP geometry instead of retaining the superseded pre-extension lock.
 741:         boolean freshGeometryActivation = p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE;
 742:         ProfitLockPolicy.State state = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(
 743:                 p.entryPrice(), p.takeProfit(), price, highest,
 744:                 freshGeometryActivation ? false : (p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE || p.profitLockActive()),
 745:                 freshGeometryActivation ? null : p.profitLockPrice(),
 746:                 settings.isDynamicProfitLockEnabled(), activation,
 747:                 nvl(settings.getProfitLockInitialPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(40)),
 748:                 nvl(settings.getProfitLockTrailStepPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(10)));
 749:         ProfitLockState nextState = state.active() ? ProfitLockState.ACTIVE : ProfitLockState.INACTIVE;
 750:         return p.withLock(state.highestPrice(), state.active(), state.lockPrice(), nextState, null);
 751:     }

 753:     private ShadowPosition rebaseForExtendedTarget(ShadowPosition p, BigDecimal newTarget, BigDecimal extensionPrice, Instant at) {
 754:         if (p == null || newTarget == null) return p;
 755:         BigDecimal oldTarget = p.takeProfit();
 756:         BigDecimal oldLock = p.profitLockPrice();
 757:         BigDecimal activation = nvl(walletSettings().getProfitLockActivationPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(70));
 758:         BigDecimal extensionHighest = p.highest() == null ? p.entryPrice() : p.highest();
 759:         if (extensionPrice != null && extensionPrice.compareTo(extensionHighest) > 0) extensionHighest = extensionPrice;
 760:         ShadowPosition withExtensionHigh = p.withLock(extensionHighest, p.profitLockActive(), p.profitLockPrice(),
 761:                 p.profitLockState(), p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt());
 762:         BigDecimal progress = progressPercent(p.entryPrice(), newTarget, extensionHighest);
 763:         if (p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE) {
 764:             if (progress.compareTo(activation) < 0) {
 765:                 ShadowPosition rebasing = withExtensionHigh.withLock(extensionHighest, true, p.profitLockPrice(), ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE,
 766:                         at == null ? Instant.now() : at);
 767:                 log.info("[FIX-120][REPLAY][TP_GEOMETRY_REBASE] positionId={}, previousState={}, newState={}, oldTarget={}, newTarget={}, " +
 768:                                 "extensionPrice={}, highest={}, newGeometryProgressPct={}, oldLock={}, freshLock={}, activationPct={}, geometryRebased=true",
 769:                         p.positionId(), p.profitLockState(), rebasing.profitLockState(), oldTarget, newTarget, extensionPrice,
 770:                         extensionHighest, progress, oldLock, rebasing.profitLockPrice(), activation);
 771:                 return rebasing;
 772:             }
 773:             // FIX-120 Production/Replay parity: the old executable lock belongs to the
 774:             // superseded TP geometry. If the extended winner still qualifies, establish a
 775:             // fresh geometry baseline. Raw extensionPrice is current; extensionHighest is the
 776:             // historical-best seed; previousActive/previousLock are deliberately discarded.
 777:             WalletSettings settings = walletSettings();
 778:             BigDecimal currentForPolicy = extensionPrice == null ? extensionHighest : extensionPrice;
 779:             ProfitLockPolicy.State rebased = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(
 780:                     p.entryPrice(), newTarget, currentForPolicy, extensionHighest,
 781:                     false, null, settings.isDynamicProfitLockEnabled(), activation,
 782:                     nvl(settings.getProfitLockInitialPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(40)),
 783:                     nvl(settings.getProfitLockTrailStepPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(10)));
 784:             if (rebased.active()) {
 785:                 ShadowPosition active = withExtensionHigh.withLock(rebased.highestPrice(), true, rebased.lockPrice(), ProfitLockState.ACTIVE, null);
 786:                 log.info("[FIX-120][REPLAY][TP_GEOMETRY_REBASE] positionId={}, previousState={}, newState={}, oldTarget={}, newTarget={}, " +
 787:                                 "extensionPrice={}, highest={}, newGeometryProgressPct={}, oldLock={}, freshLock={}, activationPct={}, geometryRebased=true",
 788:                         p.positionId(), p.profitLockState(), active.profitLockState(), oldTarget, newTarget, extensionPrice,
 789:                         rebased.highestPrice(), rebased.progressPercent(), oldLock, rebased.lockPrice(), activation);
 790:                 return active;

 668:     private FallbackNearTpEvaluation evaluateNearTpAtSignalFallback(long runId, ShadowPosition current,
 669:                                                                     TradeSignal signal, TradeSignal oneMinute,
 670:                                                                     TradeSignal fiveMinute) {
 671:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State state = new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
 672:                 current.nearTpState(), current.nearTpBestPrice(), current.nearTpBearishStreak(),
 673:                 current.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), current.nearTpHarvestUsed());
 674:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.Evaluation evaluation = nearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.evaluate(
 675:                 state, current.entryPrice(), current.takeProfit(), signal.getLatestPrice(), signal.getGeneratedAt(),
 676:                 oneMinute, fiveMinute);
 677:         ShadowPosition updated = current.withNearTp(evaluation.state());
 678:         if (!updated.sameNearTp(current)) persistNearTpState(updated);
 679:         if (evaluation.transition()) {

 681:                     runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), evaluation.code(), updated.nearTpState(),
 682:                     signal.getLatestPrice(), evaluation.givebackPercent(), updated.nearTpBearishStreak(), evaluation.explanation());
 683:         }
 684:         if (!evaluation.harvestEligible()) return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO);

 688:                 binanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.evaluate(signal.getSymbol(), soldQty, signal.getLatestPrice());

 691:                     NearTpState.NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED, updated.nearTpBestPrice(),

 695:                     runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), minimumCheck.code(), soldQty,
 696:                     minimumCheck.requestedNotional(), minimumCheck.minimumNotional());
 697:             return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO);
 698:         }
 699:         BigDecimal proceeds = soldQty.multiply(signal.getLatestPrice(), MC);
 700:         BigDecimal soldCost = updated.entryPrice().multiply(soldQty, MC);
 701:         BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(soldCost, MC);
 702:         BigDecimal pnlPct = soldCost.signum() == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO : pnl.divide(soldCost, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
 703:                 .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));

 707:                 runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), soldQty, updated.quantity(), signal.getLatestPrice(), pnl);
 708:         return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, proceeds, pnl);

 543:     private void enforceDailyLossLimit() {

 545:         BigDecimal pnl = positionRepository.sumRealizedPnlSince(startOfDay);

 139:     static boolean retryableSymbolLock(Throwable failure) {
 140:         for(Throwable e=failure;e!=null;e=e.getCause())

  21:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
  22:     public SignalProcessingFreshness(JdbcTemplate jdbc) { this.jdbc=jdbc; }
  23:     /** Production clock comes from the database session, which startup requires to be UTC. */
  24:     public boolean eligible(SignalProcessingStore.Work work) {
  25:         Timestamp databaseNow=jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)",Timestamp.class);
  26:         if(databaseNow==null)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 database did not return current timestamp");
  27:         Instant reference=databaseNow.toInstant();
  28:         if(exchangeClock!=null)reference=exchangeClock.correctDatabase(reference);
  29:         return eligible(work,reference);
  30:     }
  31:     public boolean eligible(SignalProcessingStore.Work work, Instant now) {
  32:         var candles=candleReader(jdbc).query("""
  33:                 SELECT open_time,close_time FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=?
  34:                 AND closed=1 AND close_time<=? ORDER BY open_time DESC LIMIT 1
  35:                 """,(rs,n)->new Instant[]{rs.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),rs.getTimestamp(2).toInstant()},
  36:                 work.symbol(),work.interval(),Timestamp.from(now));
  37:         if(candles.isEmpty() || !candles.get(0)[0].equals(work.candleOpenTime()))return false;
  38:         return !candles.get(0)[1].plus(grace(work.interval())).isBefore(now);
  39:     }
  40:     static Duration grace(String interval) {
  41:         return switch(interval) {
  42:             case "1m" -> Duration.ofSeconds(90);
  43:             case "5m" -> Duration.ofMinutes(2);
  44:             case "1h" -> Duration.ofMinutes(5);
  45:             case "4h" -> Duration.ofMinutes(10);
  46:             case "1d" -> Duration.ofMinutes(30);
  47:             default -> Duration.ofMinutes(2);
  48:         };
  49:     }

 122:         signal.setSignalMode("RECOVERY_EXECUTABLE");
 123:         signal = signalRepository.save(signal);

 125:         return signal;

 156:     /**
 157:      * FIX-043 production recovery path. Persists a missing historical signal while forcing
 158:      * every time-sensitive context lookup (sentiment, fundamentals, MTF/BTC context, etc.)
 159:      * to the recovered candle's original close/evaluation time.
 160:      *
 161:      * This is deliberately NOT equivalent to analyze(indicator), which uses Instant.now().
 162:      * Using current context to reconstruct a 12:21 candle at 12:30 would create a synthetic
 163:      * signal that Production could never have produced at 12:21 and would break Replay parity.
 164:      * The same buildSignal(...) implementation is shared; only temporal authority differs.
 165:      */
 166:     @Transactional
 167:     public TradeSignal analyzeRecovered(TechnicalIndicator indicator, Instant evaluationTime) {
 168:         if (indicator == null) {
 169:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Recovered technical indicator is required");
 170:         }
 171:         IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
 172:         Instant asOf = evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime;
 173:         TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, asOf, true, false);
 174:         signal.setSignalMode("HISTORICAL_ONLY");
 175:         return signalRepository.save(signal);
 176:     }

 178:     /**
 179:      * Regression-only analysis path. Runs the same production scoring/final-decision
 180:      * code but returns an unsaved TradeSignal so replay can never write to trade_signal.
 181:      */
 182:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 183:     public TradeSignal analyzeForRegression(IndicatorSnapshot snapshot, Instant evaluationTime) {
 184:         if (snapshot == null) {
 185:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Regression indicator snapshot is required");
 186:         }
 187:         ReplayAnalysisProfiler profiler = replayAnalysisProfiler.get();
 188:         long startedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 189:         try {
 190:             return buildSignal(snapshot, evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime, true, true);
 191:         } finally {
 192:             if (profiler != null) {
 193:                 profiler.recordCall(System.nanoTime() - startedNs);
 194:             }
 195:         }
 196:     }

 108:     /**
 109:      * FIX-112C historical replay path. Replay reads persisted Production Order Book
 110:      * observations strictly as-of the historical evaluation timestamp and routes them
 111:      * through the same normalized evaluator used by live Production. Pre-V74 windows
 112:      * with no persisted evidence remain explicitly UNAVAILABLE.
 113:      */
 114:     public OrderBookLiquidityResult evaluateHistorical(
 115:             String symbol,
 116:             String interval,
 117:             SignalDecision currentDecision,
 118:             boolean entryAllowed,
 119:             BigDecimal currentPrice,
 120:             BigDecimal stopLoss,
 121:             BigDecimal takeProfit,
 122:             Instant evaluatedAt
 123:     ) {
 124:         IntervalPolicy policy = properties.policyFor(interval == null ? "5m" : interval);
 125:         Instant snapshotTime = evaluatedAt == null ? Instant.now() : evaluatedAt;
 126:         if (!properties.enabled()) {
 127:             return result(LiquidityContextStatus.DISABLED, currentDecision, currentDecision,
 128:                     entryAllowed, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
 129:                     false, false, 0, "Order-book liquidity analysis is disabled.", snapshotTime,
 130:                     policy, 0L);
 131:         }
 132: 
 133:         // FIX-112C: historical Replay reads only persisted Production observations
 134:         // whose observed_at is inside the exact as-of window. No live Binance book
 135:         // and no future observation can enter a historical decision.
 136:         Instant windowStart = snapshotTime.minusSeconds(policy.windowSeconds());
 137:         List<NormalizedObservation> observations = orderBookSnapshotService
 138:                 .find(symbol, windowStart, snapshotTime).stream()
 139:                 .map(row -> new NormalizedObservation(row.observedAt(),
 140:                         // FIX-112C parity preservation: an invalid persisted snapshot must remain
 141:                         // in the observation sequence. Pre-FIX-112 Production counted every snapshot
 142:                         // in the active window and, critically, returned UNAVAILABLE when the latest
 143:                         // snapshot itself had no valid midpoint. Dropping invalid rows here would
 144:                         // silently change that established Production behavior during Replay.
 145:                         row.bestBid() == null || row.bestAsk() == null
 146:                                 || row.bestBid().add(row.bestAsk()).signum() <= 0
 147:                                 ? null
 148:                                 : new SnapshotMetrics(
 149:                                         row.bidDepth(), row.askDepth(), row.depthImbalance(),
 150:                                         row.spreadBps() == null ? null : row.spreadBps().divide(ONE_HUNDRED, MC),
 151:                                         row.bidWallPrice(), row.bidWallQuantity(),
 152:                                         row.askWallPrice(), row.askWallQuantity())))
 153:                 .toList();
 154:         if (observations.isEmpty()) {
 155:             return result(LiquidityContextStatus.UNAVAILABLE, currentDecision, currentDecision,
 156:                     entryAllowed, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
 157:                     false, false, 0,
 158:                     "No persisted Production order-book observation exists for this historical replay window; live data was not substituted.",
 159:                     snapshotTime, policy, 0L);
 160:         }
 161:         return evaluateNormalized(symbol, interval, currentDecision, entryAllowed,
 162:                 currentPrice, stopLoss, takeProfit, snapshotTime, policy, observations);
 163:     }
 164: 
 165:     public OrderBookLiquidityResult evaluate(
 166:             String symbol,
 167:             String interval,
 168:             SignalDecision currentDecision,
 169:             boolean entryAllowed,
 170:             BigDecimal currentPrice,
 171:             BigDecimal stopLoss,
 172:             BigDecimal takeProfit,
 173:             Instant evaluatedAt
 174:     ) {
 175:         IntervalPolicy policy = properties.policyFor(interval == null ? "5m" : interval);
 176:         Instant snapshotTime = evaluatedAt == null ? Instant.now() : evaluatedAt;
 177:         if (!properties.enabled()) {
 178:             return result(LiquidityContextStatus.DISABLED, currentDecision, currentDecision,
 179:                     entryAllowed, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
 180:                     false, false, 0, "Order-book liquidity analysis is disabled.", snapshotTime,
 181:                     policy, 0L);
 182:         }
 183: 
 184:         String normalizedSymbol = normalize(symbol);
 185:         Deque<OrderBookSnapshot> deque = historyBySymbol.get(normalizedSymbol);
 186:         if (deque == null || deque.isEmpty()) {
 187:             collectSafely(normalizedSymbol);
 188:             deque = historyBySymbol.get(normalizedSymbol);
 189:         }
 190:         if (deque == null || deque.isEmpty()) {
 191:             // FIX-091 / Fix 2: in LIVE analysis, zero observations are not proof that liquidity is safe.
 192:             // Hold a fresh entry until the configured sample minimum is reached. Historical replay uses
 193:             // evaluateHistorical(...) and deliberately keeps its separate UNAVAILABLE limitation.
 194:             return result(LiquidityContextStatus.INSUFFICIENT_DATA_HOLD, currentDecision, currentDecision,
 195:                     false, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
 196:                     false, false, 0, "Live order-book sampling is not ready (0/" + policy.minimumObservations()
 197:                             + "). Fresh entry is held until enough observations are collected.", snapshotTime,
 198:                     policy, 0L);
 199:         }
 200: 
 201:         List<OrderBookSnapshot> snapshots;
 202:         synchronized (deque) {
 203:             Instant windowStart = snapshotTime.minusSeconds(policy.windowSeconds());
 204:             snapshots = deque.stream()
 205:                     .filter(snapshot -> !snapshot.capturedAt().isAfter(snapshotTime))
 206:                     .filter(snapshot -> !snapshot.capturedAt().isBefore(windowStart))
 207:                     .toList();
 208:         }
 209:         if (snapshots.isEmpty()) {
 210:             // FIX-091 / Fix 2: an enabled LIVE order-book service with no observation in the active
 211:             // window is an insufficient-sampling condition, not a neutral PASS.
 212:             return result(LiquidityContextStatus.INSUFFICIENT_DATA_HOLD, currentDecision, currentDecision,
 213:                     false, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
 214:                     false, false, 0,
 215:                     "No live order-book observation exists inside the " + policy.windowSeconds()
 216:                             + " second window for interval " + interval + "; fresh entry is held until "
 217:                             + policy.minimumObservations() + " observations are available.",
 218:                     snapshotTime, policy, 0L);
 219:         }
 220: 
 221:         // FIX-112C parity preservation: keep every live snapshot in sequence and let the
 222:         // shared evaluator enforce the exact pre-refactor latest-snapshot validity gate.
 223:         // Do not filter individual invalid snapshots here; doing so changed live Production
 224:         // semantics by allowing an older valid snapshot to replace an invalid latest reading.
 225:         List<NormalizedObservation> observations = snapshots.stream()
 226:                 .map(snapshot -> {
 227:                     BigDecimal mid = midpoint(snapshot);
 228:                     return new NormalizedObservation(snapshot.capturedAt(),
 229:                             mid == null || mid.signum() <= 0 ? null : metrics(snapshot, mid));
 230:                 })
 231:                 .toList();
 232:         return evaluateNormalized(symbol, interval, currentDecision, entryAllowed,
 233:                 currentPrice, stopLoss, takeProfit, snapshotTime, policy, observations);
 234:     }

 240:      */
 241:     private OrderBookLiquidityResult evaluateNormalized(

 243:             BigDecimal currentPrice, BigDecimal stopLoss, BigDecimal takeProfit,
 244:             Instant snapshotTime, IntervalPolicy policy,
 245:             List<NormalizedObservation> observations) {

  39:                                                             boolean entryAllowed, Instant evaluatedAt) {

  41:         if (!properties.enabled()) {
  42:             return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
  43:                     null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), 0,
  44:                     "Derivatives positioning is disabled.", time);
  45:         }
  46:         return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.UNAVAILABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
  47:                 null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), 0,

  51: 
  52:     public DerivativesPositioningResult evaluate(String symbol, String interval, SignalDecision decision,
  53:                                                   boolean entryAllowed, Instant evaluatedAt) {
  54:         Instant now = evaluatedAt == null ? Instant.now() : evaluatedAt;
  55:         if (!properties.enabled()) return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
  56:                 null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), 0,
  57:                 "Derivatives positioning is disabled.", now);
  58:         try {
  59:             List<BinanceFundingRate> funding = client.fundingHistory(symbol, properties.fundingHistoryLimit());
  60:             List<BinanceOpenInterestPoint> oi = client.openInterestHistory(symbol, period(interval), properties.openInterestHistoryLimit());
  61:             if (funding.isEmpty() || oi.size() < 2) {
  62:                 return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.LEARNING, decision, entryAllowed,
  63:                         latestFunding(funding), percentile(funding), latestOi(oi), latestOiValue(oi), null,
  64:                         priceChange(symbol, interval), funding.size(), period(interval), -5,
  65:                         "Funding or open-interest history is still insufficient; no veto was applied.", now);
  66:             }
  67:             BigDecimal fundingRate = latestFunding(funding);
  68:             BigDecimal fundingPercentile = percentile(funding);
  69:             BigDecimal oiChange = percentChange(oi.get(oi.size() - 2).sumOpenInterest(), oi.get(oi.size() - 1).sumOpenInterest());
  70:             BigDecimal priceChange = priceChange(symbol, interval);
  71:             DerivativesPositioningStatus status = classify(fundingRate, oiChange, priceChange);
  72:             SignalDecision finalDecision = decision;
  73:             boolean allowed = entryAllowed;
  74:             int confidence = confidence(status);
  75:             if (isBullish(decision) && status == DerivativesPositioningStatus.LONGS_CROWDED
  76:                     && properties.vetoExtremeCrowding()
  77:                     && oiChange != null && oiChange.abs().compareTo(properties.strongOpenInterestChangeThreshold()) >= 0) {
  78:                 finalDecision = SignalDecision.WATCH;
  79:                 allowed = false;
  80:             }
  81:             if (isBearish(decision) && status == DerivativesPositioningStatus.SHORTS_CROWDED
  82:                     && properties.vetoExtremeCrowding()
  83:                     && oiChange != null && oiChange.abs().compareTo(properties.strongOpenInterestChangeThreshold()) >= 0) {
  84:                 finalDecision = SignalDecision.NEUTRAL;
  85:                 allowed = false;
  86:             }
  87:             String explanation = explanation(status, fundingRate, fundingPercentile, oiChange, priceChange, allowed);
  88:             BinanceOpenInterestPoint latest = oi.get(oi.size() - 1);
  89:             return result(status, finalDecision, allowed, fundingRate, fundingPercentile,
  90:                     latest.sumOpenInterest(), latest.sumOpenInterestValue(), oiChange, priceChange,
  91:                     funding.size(), period(interval), confidence, explanation, now);
  92:         } catch (RestClientResponseException ex) {
  93:             if (ex.getStatusCode().value() == 400) {
  94:                 return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
  95:                         null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), 0,
  96:                         "No matching Binance USD-M perpetual contract is available for " + symbol + ".", now);
  97:             }
  98:             return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.UNAVAILABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
  99:                     null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), -10,
 100:                     "Binance futures positioning data is unavailable: " + ex.getMessage(), now);
 101:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 102:             return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.UNAVAILABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
 103:                     null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), -10,
 104:                     "Binance futures positioning data is unavailable: " + ex.getMessage(), now);
 105:         }
 106:     }
 107: 
 108:     private DerivativesPositioningStatus classify(BigDecimal funding, BigDecimal oiChange, BigDecimal priceChange) {
 109:         if (funding != null && funding.compareTo(properties.extremeFundingRate()) >= 0) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.LONGS_CROWDED;

 209:     private int rawConfidence(
 210:             MarketRegimeAssessment regime,
 211:             MarketContextSnapshot context,
 212:             MultiTimeframeConfluenceResult confluence,
 213:             BtcMarketContextResult btc,
 214:             DerivativesPositioningResult derivatives,
 215:             OrderBookLiquidityResult liquidity
 216:     ) {
 217:         double value = regime.confidence() * 0.25;
 218:         value += (context.dataValid() ? 100 : 0) * 0.15;
 219:         value += confluenceConfidence(confluence.status()) * 0.20;
 220:         value += btcConfidence(btc.contextStatus(), btc.stable()) * 0.15;
 221:         value += liquidityConfidence(liquidity.status(), liquidity.observations()) * 0.12;
 222:         value += derivativesConfidence(derivatives) * 0.08;
 223:         value += sentimentConfidence(context) * 0.10;
 224:         int result = (int) Math.round(value);
 225:         return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, result));
 226:     }

 228:     private int confluenceConfidence(ConfluenceStatus status) {
 229:         return switch (status) {
 230:             case STRONG_AGREEMENT -> 100;
 231:             case AGREEMENT -> 90;
 232:             case MIXED -> 60;
 233:             case CONFLICT -> 30;
 234:             case STRONG_CONFLICT -> 10;
 235:             case UNAVAILABLE -> 40;
 236:         };
 237:     }

 239:     private int btcConfidence(BtcContextStatus status, boolean stable) {
 240:         int base = switch (status) {
 241:             case CONFIRMED -> 95;
 242:             case NOT_APPLICABLE -> 85;
 243:             case NEUTRAL -> 75;
 244:             case LEARNING -> 50;
 245:             case CONFLICT -> 30;
 246:             case STRONG_CONFLICT -> 10;
 247:             case UNAVAILABLE -> 40;
 248:             case STALE_CONTEXT -> 0;
 249:         };
 250:         return stable ? base : Math.max(0, base - 10);
 251:     }
 252: 
 253:     private int liquidityConfidence(LiquidityContextStatus status, int observations) {
 254:         int base = switch (status) {
 255:             case BULLISH_SUPPORT -> 95;
 256:             case BALANCED, DISABLED -> 85;
 257:             case LEARNING -> 55;
 258:             case INSUFFICIENT_DATA_HOLD -> 25;
 259:             case UNAVAILABLE -> 40;
 260:             case BEARISH_PRESSURE, STOP_EXPOSED -> 35;
 261:             case TARGET_BLOCKED, THIN_LIQUIDITY -> 15;
 262:             case WALL_WEAKENING -> 65;
 263:         };
 264:         if (observations <= 0 && status != LiquidityContextStatus.DISABLED) {
 265:             return Math.min(base, 40);
 266:         }
 267:         return base;
 268:     }

 270:     private int derivativesConfidence(DerivativesPositioningResult result) {
 271:         int base = switch (result.status()) {
 272:             case FRESH_LONG_BUILDUP, FRESH_SHORT_BUILDUP -> 95;
 273:             case HEALTHY_BULLISH, HEALTHY_BEARISH, BALANCED, NOT_APPLICABLE -> 80;

 275:             case LONGS_CROWDED, SHORTS_CROWDED -> 25;
 276:             case LEARNING -> 50;
 277:             case UNAVAILABLE -> 40;
 278:         };
 279:         return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, base + result.confidenceAdjustment()));
 280:     }

--- src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.java:25-60 ---
  25: @Slf4j
  26: public class BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy {
  27:     private static final MathContext MC = MathContext.DECIMAL64;
  28: 
  29:     private final BinanceMarketDataClient binanceMarketDataClient;
  30:     public Evaluation evaluate(String symbol, BigDecimal quantity, BigDecimal price) {
  31:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank() || quantity == null || quantity.signum() <= 0
  32:                 || price == null || price.signum() <= 0) {
  33:             return new Evaluation(false, "INVALID_ORDER_INPUT", BigDecimal.ZERO, null);
  34:         }
  35:         String pair = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  36:         BigDecimal notional = quantity.multiply(price, MC);
  37:         BigDecimal minimum;
  38:         try {
  39:             minimum = binanceMarketDataClient.getMinimumExecutableNotional(pair);
  40:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
  41:             // Fail safe. Missing exchange constraints must never be converted into permission
  42:             // to create an order that real Binance might reject.
  43:             log.warn("FIX-11T Binance minimum executable amount unavailable: symbol={}, requestedNotional={}, error={}",
  44:                     pair, notional, ex.getMessage());
  45:             return new Evaluation(false, "BINANCE_MINIMUM_UNAVAILABLE", notional, null);
  46:         }
  47:         if (notional.compareTo(minimum) < 0) {
  48:             return new Evaluation(false, "BELOW_BINANCE_MINIMUM", notional, minimum);
  49:         }
  50:         return new Evaluation(true, "EXECUTABLE", notional, minimum);
  51:     }
  52: 
  53:     public record Evaluation(boolean executable, String code, BigDecimal requestedNotional, BigDecimal minimumNotional) {
  54:     }
  55: }

 152: 
 153:     /**
 154:      * FIX-11T: read the symbol's current Binance minimum executable notional directly
 155:      * from exchangeInfo.  Near-TP uses this only as a minimum-order guard; it does not
 156:      * introduce a separate quantity-rounding model. NOTIONAL is preferred when present,
 157:      * with MIN_NOTIONAL retained for symbols that still expose the older filter.
 158:      */
 159:     public BigDecimal getMinimumExecutableNotional(String symbol) {
 160:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank()) {
 161:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol is required");
 162:         }
 163: 
 164:         Map<String, Object> response = request(() -> restClient.get()
 165:             .uri(uriBuilder -> uriBuilder
 166:                 .path("/api/v3/exchangeInfo")
 167:                 .queryParam("symbol", symbol.toUpperCase())
 168:                 .build())
 169:             .retrieve()
 170:             .body(new ParameterizedTypeReference<>() {
 171:             }));
 172: 
 173:         if (response == null) {
 174:             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty Binance exchangeInfo response for " + symbol);
 175:         }
 176:         Object symbolsValue = response.get("symbols");
 177:         if (!(symbolsValue instanceof List<?> symbols) || symbols.isEmpty() || !(symbols.get(0) instanceof Map<?, ?> symbolInfo)) {
 178:             throw new IllegalStateException("Binance exchangeInfo did not contain symbol rules for " + symbol);
 179:         }
 180:         Object filtersValue = symbolInfo.get("filters");
 181:         if (!(filtersValue instanceof List<?> filters)) {
 182:             throw new IllegalStateException("Binance exchangeInfo did not contain filters for " + symbol);
 183:         }
 184: 
 185:         BigDecimal minNotional = null;
 186:         for (Object filterValue : filters) {
 187:             if (!(filterValue instanceof Map<?, ?> filter)) continue;
 188:             String filterType = String.valueOf(filter.get("filterType"));
 189:             if (!"NOTIONAL".equals(filterType) && !"MIN_NOTIONAL".equals(filterType)) continue;
 190:             Object raw = filter.get("minNotional");
 191:             if (raw == null) continue;
 192:             BigDecimal candidate = new BigDecimal(String.valueOf(raw));
 193:             if (candidate.signum() <= 0) continue;
 194:             if ("NOTIONAL".equals(filterType)) return candidate;
 195:             minNotional = candidate;
 196:         }
 197:         if (minNotional != null) return minNotional;
 198:         throw new IllegalStateException("Binance exchangeInfo did not expose a minimum notional for " + symbol);
 199:     }
 200: 
 201:     private BinanceKline mapKline(List<Object> row) {
 202:         if (row == null || row.size() < 11) {
 203:             throw new IllegalArgumentException(
 204:                 "Invalid Binance kline response"
 205:             );
 206:         }
 207: 
 208:         return new BinanceKline(
 209:             Instant.ofEpochMilli(toLong(row.get(0))),
 210:             toDecimal(row.get(1)),

 106:     private Optional<PaperPosition> processSignalBody(TradeSignal signal) {
 107:         if (signal == null) {
 108:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Trade signal is required");
 109:         }

 668:     private FallbackNearTpEvaluation evaluateNearTpAtSignalFallback(long runId, ShadowPosition current,
 669:                                                                     TradeSignal signal, TradeSignal oneMinute,
 670:                                                                     TradeSignal fiveMinute) {
 671:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State state = new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
 672:                 current.nearTpState(), current.nearTpBestPrice(), current.nearTpBearishStreak(),
 673:                 current.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), current.nearTpHarvestUsed());
 674:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.Evaluation evaluation = nearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.evaluate(
 675:                 state, current.entryPrice(), current.takeProfit(), signal.getLatestPrice(), signal.getGeneratedAt(),
 676:                 oneMinute, fiveMinute);
 677:         ShadowPosition updated = current.withNearTp(evaluation.state());
 678:         if (!updated.sameNearTp(current)) persistNearTpState(updated);
 679:         if (evaluation.transition()) {
 680:             log.info("FIX-11T Replay fallback Near-TP: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, code={}, state={}, price={}, givebackPct={}, bearish1mStreak={}, detail={}",
 681:                     runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), evaluation.code(), updated.nearTpState(),
 682:                     signal.getLatestPrice(), evaluation.givebackPercent(), updated.nearTpBearishStreak(), evaluation.explanation());
 683:         }
 684:         if (!evaluation.harvestEligible()) return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO);
 685: 
 686:         BigDecimal soldQty = updated.quantity().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC);
 687:         BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.Evaluation minimumCheck =
 688:                 binanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.evaluate(signal.getSymbol(), soldQty, signal.getLatestPrice());
 689:         if (!minimumCheck.executable()) {
 690:             updated = updated.withNearTp(new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
 691:                     NearTpState.NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED, updated.nearTpBestPrice(),
 692:                     0, updated.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), false));
 693:             persistNearTpState(updated);
 694:             log.info("FIX-11T Replay fallback NEAR_TP_HARVEST_SKIPPED: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, reason={}, requestedQty={}, requestedNotional={}, binanceMinimum={}; existing position management continues",
 695:                     runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), minimumCheck.code(), soldQty,
 696:                     minimumCheck.requestedNotional(), minimumCheck.minimumNotional());
 697:             return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO);
 698:         }
 699:         BigDecimal proceeds = soldQty.multiply(signal.getLatestPrice(), MC);
 700:         BigDecimal soldCost = updated.entryPrice().multiply(soldQty, MC);
 701:         BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(soldCost, MC);
 702:         BigDecimal pnlPct = soldCost.signum() == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO : pnl.divide(soldCost, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
 703:                 .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
 704:         updated = updated.withPartialHarvest(soldQty, soldCost, pnl);

 259:         // Golden rule: Replay = Production. Replay reaches this exact shared method
 260:         // through ExecutionReplayScope historical 5m/1h lookups; there is no Replay-only

 435:     private void audit(TradeSignal referenceSignal, String path,
 436:                        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment, String finalResult, String reason) {
 437:         if (htfAuditStore == null) return;
 438:         try {
 439:             htfAuditStore.record(referenceSignal, path, assessment, finalResult, reason);
 440:         } catch (RuntimeException auditFailure) {
 441:             if (fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.auditWriteFailed();
 442:             log.warn("FIX-154 audit handoff failed; BUY decision remains unchanged: path={}, signalId={}",
 443:                     path, referenceSignal == null ? null : referenceSignal.getId(), auditFailure);
 444:         }
 445:     }

 449:                 && assessment.band() == HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Band.NOT_TRUSTED) {
 450:             fix154Metrics.untrustedBearishOneHourBlocked();
 451:         }
 452:     }

  38:         return read.execute(status -> jdbc.query("""
  39:                 SELECT id, executed_at FROM wallet_trade
  40:                 WHERE symbol=? AND status='EXECUTED' AND side='SELL'
  41:                   AND execution_reason='STOP_LOSS' AND executed_at<=?
  42:                 ORDER BY executed_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1
  43:                 """, (rs, row) -> new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(rs.getLong(1),
  44:                 rs.getTimestamp(2).toInstant()), symbol, Timestamp.from(asOf))

  46:     }

  48:     public void append(Long runId, String symbol, Long signalId, Instant at, String stage, String payload) {
  49:         // Separate audit transaction survives a caller rollback; it records an evaluation,
  50:         // never asserts that a BUY/SELL actually executed. No FK to wallet rows is acquired.
  51:         write.executeWithoutResult(status -> jdbc.update("""
  52:                 INSERT INTO fix122_evaluation(test_run_id,symbol,signal_id,evaluated_at,stage,payload)
  53:                 VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)
  54:                 """, runId, symbol, signalId, Timestamp.from(at), stage, payload));
  55:     }

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The response correctly preserves RPR-0.4’s rejection and does not misrepresent itself as an amendment or approval. Its CR-01, CR-02, CR-08, and CR-09 are valid requirements for a new complete specification. [Response](C:\workspace\crypto-ai-trader-mysql\agent-docs\RPR-0.4_REVIEW_RESPONSE.md:9)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The response is not a sufficient requirements basis for the next specification. It drops the critical pre-write contradiction: current Replay persists generated signals before validating price evidence. The next specification must require whole-window qualification before any generated-signal, shadow-wallet, or certificate write—or an explicitly isolated in-memory preflight. [Worker](C:\workspace\crypto-ai-trader-mysql\src\main\java\com\crypto\regression\service\RegressionTestWorker.java:174) [signal write](C:\workspace\crypto-ai-trader-mysql\src\main\java\com\crypto\regression\service\RegressionTestWorker.java:636) [guard](C:\workspace\crypto-ai-trader-mysql\src\main\java\com\crypto\regression\service\RegressionTestWorker.java:195)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Current Replay violates the stated no-live-HTTP parity-run objective. Both exact-price and fallback near-TP paths call the minimum-execution policy, which requests current Binance `exchangeInfo`. The next specification needs a complete deny-by-default outbound-I/O inventory, immutable constraints, and a test that fails on any outbound client invocation. [Exact path](C:\workspace\crypto-ai-trader-mysql\src\main\java\com\crypto\regression\service\ShadowProductionReplayService.java:534) [fallback](C:\workspace\crypto-ai-trader-mysql\src\main\java\com\crypto\regression\service\ShadowProductionReplayService.java:686) [HTTP request](C:\workspace\crypto-ai-trader-mysql\src\main\java\com\crypto\client\binance\BinanceMarketDataClient.java:159)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** CR-03 correctly identifies important orchestration gaps, but remains incomplete. Exact-price Replay skips the signal-path TP, label-free profit-lock exit, stop-loss, and rebase hard-floor paths; it implements only a validated bearish 1m SELL afterward. Production applies all of those earlier signal-path checks. It also does not register execution expiry/lock-result outcomes or global wallet gates as required parity dimensions. [Production chain](C:\workspace\crypto-ai-trader-mysql\src\main\java\com\crypto\service\PaperTradingService.java:170) [Replay exact-price branch](C:\workspace\crypto-ai-trader-mysql\src\main\java\com\crypto\regression\service\ShadowProductionReplayService.java:232) [expiry](C:\workspace\crypto-ai-trader-mysql\src\main\java\com\crypto\execution\processing\SignalProcessingCoordinator.java:97) [daily-loss gate](C:\workspace\crypto-ai-trader-mysql\src\main\java\com\crypto\service\PaperTradingService.java:543)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** CR-04 has the right three-category shape, but no usable oracle contract. The new specification must register, per decision/execution/exit outcome: durable source, identity, as-of time, availability time, compared fields, equality rule, and the outcome where evidence is absent. `ReplayParityValidator` cannot supply this alone: it is signal-level and has no Java caller. [Validator](C:\workspace\crypto-ai-trader-mysql\src\main\java\com\crypto\regression\service\ReplayParityValidator.java:15)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Price-event ordering remains underspecified. The database query orders by `observed_at, id`, but the returned `PriceEvent` drops both ID and ordering slot; Replay then sorts only by time. Equal-time accepted observations therefore cannot be deterministically reproduced or certified. The next specification must retain immutable event IDs, ordering slots, source, and a legacy tie/refusal rule. [Query and record](C:\workspace\crypto-ai-trader-mysql\src\main\java\com\crypto\market\service\MarketPriceEventService.java:118) [Replay sort](C:\workspace\crypto-ai-trader-mysql\src\main\java\com\crypto\regression\service\ShadowProductionReplayService.java:89)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** CR-05 correctly treats `NON_CANONICAL` and `PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED` as excluded from the accepted protection stream, but it does not define protection coverage or the separate candle-signal path. Production can update lock state and close positions from `signal.latestPrice`, independently of a price-event row. The next specification must qualify exclusions as protection-stream exclusions only, define accepted-event coverage while a position is open, and model the signal-price exit chain separately. [Accepted-event filter](C:\workspace\crypto-ai-trader-mysql\src\main\java\com\crypto\market\service\MarketPriceEventService.java:123) [signal exit path](C:\workspace\crypto-ai-trader-mysql\src\main\java\com\crypto\service\PaperTradingService.java:170)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The response omits the material live-versus-historical order-book inversion. With no live observations, Production attempts collection then returns `INSUFFICIENT_DATA_HOLD` and blocks entry; historical Replay returns `UNAVAILABLE` while retaining the incoming entry permission. Stored rows alone do not prove the live in-memory window was complete. A parity run must require a durable completeness/availability contract or refuse. [Replay](C:\workspace\crypto-ai-trader-mysql\src\main\java\com\crypto\service\OrderBookLiquidityService.java:154) [Production](C:\workspace\crypto-ai-trader-mysql\src\main\java\com\crypto\service\OrderBookLiquidityService.java:184)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** CR-06 is directionally correct but incomplete. Stored Production signals contain substantial derivatives/order-book outputs, but the specification must choose field-by-field among sufficient stored-evidence injection, a clearly labelled reconstruction, or refusal. Reconstruction from external history cannot establish what Production observed. Further, historical Replay’s `UNAVAILABLE` has adjustment `0`, whereas Production’s failed live call returns `UNAVAILABLE` with `-10`; this can affect confidence. The working tree contains V107/V109 and `TradeExecutionValidationService` invokes higher-timeframe assessment in shared BUY validation. This proves the source state, not deployment. ## EVIDENCE

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The complete next specification must include registered requirements for:

  1. Full working-tree baseline identity, deployment artefact/configuration fingerprint, migration/Flyway policy, and resolution of the FIX-154 source-versus-documentation contradiction.
  2. Pre-write whole-window eligibility and a no-write refusal test.
  3. A decision, execution, protection, exit, and work-outcome oracle registry.
  4. Immutable replay account/configuration/constraint snapshots; clear wallet/position read boundaries.
  5. Zero outbound HTTP, plus inventory of mutable configuration, database reads, audit writes, and in-process side effects.
  6. Price-event lineage, coverage, ordering, tie rules, source-era rules, and candle-signal-path treatment.
  7. Context evidence rules for derivatives, order book, sentiment, fundamentals, BTC, and higher timeframes, including availability time and field-sufficiency proof.
  8. Full orchestration: signal exits, direct protection, fills, freshness expiry, retry/lock outcomes, daily clock, limits, progressive adds, and cancellation/resource isolation.
  9. An order-book completeness/refusal policy.
  10. Single-symbol-first scope unless deterministic multi-symbol account scheduling is fully specified.
  11. A retirement decision table: observers must be suppressed in baseline parity now; deletion remains a separate user-approved decision.
  12. Production regression, disposable-MySQL migration, no-look-ahead, zero-I/O, refusal, resource/cancellation, random-window, and incident-window validation.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The local YAML values are explicitly local-test defaults and can be overridden. They cannot be presented as historical Production settings. [Configuration](C:\workspace\crypto-ai-trader-mysql\src\main\resources\application.yml:1) [limits](C:\workspace\crypto-ai-trader-mysql\src\main\resources\application.yml:120)

## OBJECTIONS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Reject the response as a complete requirements basis because it omits pre-write qualification, a concrete oracle registry, deterministic price-event lineage, the order-book inversion/completeness condition, and multiple execution paths.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The response’s recommendation to reset `RegimeStateService` is unsupported as a material parity requirement in the reviewed response. It should not be carried into the next contract unless a complete reference search proves an active Replay state leak and defines its impact.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** “Lifecycle parity” should remain unavailable unless immutable evidence both establishes a matching virtual starting state and shows historical global constraints were reproduced or demonstrably non-binding. Otherwise the permitted result is decision parity, simulation, or refusal.

## UNKNOWNS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** No database, production system, runtime log, deployment record, build, or test was accessed or executed in this review.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The deployed JAR, deployed configuration, actual Flyway history, dates of evidence eras, and whether FIX-154 is deployed remain unverified.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Older periods will often be ineligible for full parity unless durable historical context, configuration, and account-constraint evidence can be demonstrated sufficient.
